Together, these results indicate that frozen TSFMs already support dynamic in-context use of retrievals, and that closed-form alignment should be the default baseline for retrieval-augmented forecasting before any fusion module is trained. Code available at: https://github.com/masadi-99/align-rag

</details>


### [239] [LC-GRPO: Bridging Train-Inference Gap for Flow-Based GRPO with Langevin Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05600)
*Yingqing Guo,Hui Yuan,Zijian He,Mengdi Wang,Zheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LC-GRPO框架，通过在每个训练回合中先执行ODE Euler步再进行朗之万校正，解决流模型GRPO训练中SDE rollout与测试时ODE采样器之间的分布不匹配问题，无需额外训练score模型，并在多个文生图和文生视频模型上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流模型通常用确定性ODE采样，但GRPO在线强化学习需要随机rollout进行策略探索，现有方法使用SDE替代ODE导致训练与测试之间的分布失配，且SDE rollout在噪声增大时变得模糊。

Method: LC-GRPO的核心思想是：每个rollout转移先执行与推理对齐的ODE Euler步，然后施加一个随机朗之万校正，使样本对准该时间步的边际分布。score直接从流速度中恢复，无需额外score模型；所得转移保持各向同性高斯分布，具有可处理的似然用于策略优化。

Result: 理论分析表明，在适当条件下，一次朗之万校正步可降低不完美ODE Euler步的Wasserstein误差；在匹配随机性水平下，所提转移比标准Euler-Maruyama离散化的反向SDE更精确。实验在SD3.5-Medium、FLUX.1-Dev和HunyuanVideo上一致提升了奖励优化，保持了生成质量，并大幅缩小了训练rollout与测试ODE推理之间的差距。

Conclusion: LC-GRPO通过朗之万校正有效弥合了随机训练rollout与确定性测试推理之间的差距，既保持了流模型GRPO训练的探索能力，又确保了与推理时ODE采样器的一致性。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are typically sampled by solving a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE), whereas online reinforcement learning requires stochastic rollouts for policy exploration and optimization. Existing GRPO methods for flow models therefore replace the inference-time ODE with a stochastic differential equation (SDE) during training. Although the ODE and SDE share the same marginal distributions in continuous time, their finite-step discretizations can differ substantially. In particular, SDE rollouts often become blurry as the exploration noise increases, creating a mismatch between the samples used for reinforcement learning and those generated by the test-time ODE sampler. We introduce LC-GRPO, a flow-based GRPO framework with Langevin correction. Each rollout transition first takes an inference-aligned ODE Euler step and then applies a stochastic Langevin correction targeting the marginal distribution at the resulting timestep. The required score is recovered directly from the flow velocity, requiring no additional score model, while the resulting transition remains an isotropic Gaussian with a tractable likelihood for policy optimization. We theoretically show that, under suitable conditions, one Langevin correction step reduces the Wasserstein error of an imperfect ODE Euler step. At a matched randomness level, we further show that the proposed transition can be more accurate than the standard Euler--Maruyama discretization of the reverse SDE. Experiments on SD3.5-Medium, FLUX.1-Dev, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LC-GRPO consistently improves reward optimization across text-to-image and text-to-video tasks, preserves generation quality, and substantially narrows the gap between stochastic training rollouts and deterministic test-time ODE inference.

</details>


### [240] [GAUGE: Granularity-Adaptive Counterfactual Gating of Evidence for Incomplete Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05608)
*Yunping Shi,En Yu,Kairui Guo,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GAUGE，一个轻量级的反事实门控框架，用于处理多模态分类中的模态不完整问题。通过泰勒证据分数对细粒度证据单元进行反事实效应评分，并转化为连续门控实现对证据的单元级调制，在六个基准上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在不完整多模态分类中通常在粗粒度的模态层面处理缺失，无法在同一恢复模态内保留可靠成分同时抑制误导成分，导致预测可靠性受损。

Method: GAUGE首先用冻结的插补器补全缺失模态，将观测和恢复的输入统一编码为细粒度证据单元。通过预测感知的泰勒证据分数在单次前向-后向传播中计算每个单元替换为参考表征的反事实效应，将分数映射为连续门控并转化为加性注意力对数偏置，实现单元级证据调制，无需改变骨干网络架构。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，GAUGE在各种不完整输入设置下优于强基线方法。此外，泰勒余项理论分析刻画了一阶近似相对于精确反事实效应的误差。

Conclusion: GAUGE为模态不完整下的细粒度证据控制提供了有原则且可扩展的框架，兼具理论保证和实际有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal classification typically assumes all modalities are available, yet real-world inputs are often incomplete. Imputation and dynamic fusion can mitigate such incompleteness, but existing methods operate at a coarse modality level and thus cannot retain reliable components while suppressing misleading ones within the same recovered modality, compromising prediction reliability. To address this issue, we propose GAUGE, a lightweight counterfactual gating framework for incomplete multimodal classification. GAUGE first imputes missing modalities with a frozen imputer and encodes observed and recovered inputs uniformly as fine-grained evidence units. Rather than intervening on each unit explicitly, GAUGE scores the counterfactual effect of replacing every unit with a reference representation through prediction-aware Taylor evidence scores, all obtained in a single forward-backward pass. These scores are mapped to continuous gates, which are converted into additive attention-logit biases for unit-wise evidence modulation without altering the backbone architecture. Experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate that GAUGE outperforms strong baselines across diverse incomplete-input settings. Furthermore, a Taylor remainder theoretical analysis characterizes the error of the first-order approximation relative to the exact counterfactual effect, establishing GAUGE as a principled and scalable framework for fine-grained evidence control under modality incompleteness.

</details>


### [241] [Reasoning Errors Have a Region and a Direction in the Residual-Stream Trajectory of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05660)
*Hamed Damirchi,Ignacio Meza De la Jara,Damith Ranasinghe,Yuhang Liu,Javen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三流检测器，结合轨迹运动（motion）、粗粒度区域信息（region）和细粒度方向信息（direction）来判别语言模型推理的正确性，在未见过的推理基准上相比仅用位移的方法提升了最高12%的选择准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于轨迹（trajectory-based）的推理正确性检测方法只使用层间残差位移（displacement），但位移忽略了状态更新前的上下文信息；而恢复完整状态又会重新引入易受捷径影响的信息，存在两难权衡。

Method: 提出三流检测器：1）运动流——层间残差位移，捕捉表征变化；2）粗粒度区域读取器——基于向量量化（vector quantization）读取位置的粗略区域信息；3）细粒度方向读取器——在归一化的多层状态上读取方向信息。三者结合，在不回到完整状态探测的前提下恢复足够的状态上下文来解读运动。

Result: 在训练时未见过的推理基准上，相比仅用位移的最先进方法提升最高12%的选择准确率，相比单层探测基线提升21%；在事实补全和事实验证任务上表现也优于所有对比检测器；消融实验表明运动、区域和方向三种信号互补。

Conclusion: 推理有效性更适合通过状态条件化的运动（state-conditioned motion）来读取，而非静态状态或去上下文化的轨迹单独判断；该信号反映的是正确性本身，而非特定推理类型。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly used for tasks that require verifiable reasoning, reliably distinguishing sound reasoning from flawed reasoning has become an important practical problem. Recent trajectory-based methods seek this signal in layerwise residual-stream displacements, which capture how representations change while attenuating some stable, token-specific information. However, displacement omits the state from which an update originates, whereas restoring the full state risks reintroducing shortcut-prone information. We identify this trade-off and propose a three-stream detector that combines motion with two restricted views of location. A coarse region reader based on vector quantization and a fine direction reader over normalized multi-layer states. This design restores enough state context to interpret the motion without returning to full-state probing. On reasoning benchmarks unseen during training, our method improves selection accuracy by up to 12% over the displacement-only state of the art and 21% over single-layer probing baselines. Although trained only on reasoning benchmarks, it also reads factual completion and fact verification, ahead of every detector we compare against, which places the signal on correctness rather than on a kind of reasoning. Ablations further show that motion, region, and direction provide complementary signals. These results suggest that reasoning validity is better read from state-conditioned motion than from either static states or decontextualized trajectories alone.

</details>


### [242] [Potential Matching Optimal Transport: Continuous Normalizing Flows for Exact $p$-Wasserstein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05666)
*Lishuo Zhang,Ruizhi Huang,Yang Yu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PMOT（势匹配最优传输）框架，用于一般p-代价最优传输问题（c_p(x,y)=||x-y||^p）。PMOT用标量势参数化连续归一化流（CNF）的速度场，采用广义Benamou-Brenier形式，通过自诱导匹配损失沿直线桥训练势梯度，并允许灵活的终端分布匹配。核心结果证明零损失精确性：在正则性、精确终端匹配和唯一性假设下，任何零损失解都满足广义Benamou-Brenier最优性系统并恢复对应的p-最优传输映射和动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输方法大多局限于特定代价函数（如2-代价），缺乏对一般p-代价最优传输问题的统一可扩展框架。作者希望构建一个能够处理任意p-代价的连续归一化流框架，同时保证理论上的最优性保证和实际上的计算可行性。

Method: PMOT将连续归一化流（CNF）的速度场参数化为标量势的梯度，采用广义Benamou-Brenier形式（针对所选指数p）。训练过程使用自诱导匹配损失，沿直线桥（straight bridges）由模型自身端点确定路径，同时允许终端分布的灵活匹配（如基于MMD的样本匹配）。

Result: 在合成基准上，PMOT学习到的p-特定映射与对应的p-匹配最优传输参考一致。在高维表格数据上作为基于似然的密度模型保持竞争力。基于MMD的颜色变换实验展示了灵活的基于样本的终端匹配能力。

Conclusion: PMOT提供了统一的p-代价最优传输学习框架，兼具理论保证（零损失下恢复最优传输映射的精确性）和实际灵活性（多样化的终端匹配方式），在合成数据、高维密度建模和颜色变换等任务中均表现良好。

Abstract: We introduce Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT), a potential-flow framework for general $p$-cost optimal transport with $c_p(x,y)=\|x-y\|^p$. PMOT parameterizes the CNF velocity field with a scalar potential in the generalized Benamou--Brenier form for the chosen exponent $p$. It trains the potential gradient with a self-induced matching loss along straight bridges determined by the model's own endpoints, while allowing flexible terminal distribution matching. Our main result establishes zero-loss exactness: under the stated regularity, exact terminal matching, and uniqueness assumptions, any zero-loss solution satisfies the generalized Benamou--Brenier optimality system and recovers the corresponding $p$-optimal transport map and dynamics. On synthetic benchmarks, PMOT learns $p$-specific maps that agree with the corresponding $p$-matched OT references. It also remains competitive as a likelihood-based density model on high-dimensional tabular data, and an MMD-based color transformation experiment demonstrates flexible sample-based terminal matching.

</details>


### [243] [When Does Consensus Mean Correctness? Measuring the Agreement-Accuracy Coupling with Semantics-Preserving Re-Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05670)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过科学图表数据构建RENDEQ渲染等价数据集，系统测量了视觉语言模型在扰动输入下的一致性（agreement）与正确性（correctness）之间的耦合关系。研究发现重渲染优于重采样，但用模型自身跨渲染一致性进行微调反而降低准确性，表明一致性认证正确性存在阈值条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型在扰动输入上的一致性常被用作无标签可靠性信号和自训练目标，但其与正确性的耦合关系很少被直接测量。自然图像扰动难以保证语义等价比且缺乏精确答案，而科学图表（由程序绘制）恰好同时解决了这两个障碍，为验证这一关键假设提供了理想实验环境。

Method: 构建RENDEQ——一个生成渲染等价数据集（render-equivalence sets）的生成器；在三个开放权重VLM上、三个独立实例化中测量一致性-正确性耦合；比较重渲染与重采样的效果；以平均token对数概率作为证据携带基线进行对比；并测试基于模型自身跨渲染共识进行微调的效果。

Result: 重渲染在准确性和可靠性上均优于重采样；一致性在三个模型中两个优于基线（平均token对数概率）、一个持平。方差分析显示绘图库这一风格因素贡献的方差是次大因素的2倍多、比噪声底高一个数量级。用模型自身跨渲染共识微调后，五次复现中准确率全部下降，与自然图像上的发表结果符号相反。

Conclusion: 一致性仅在一个阈值之上才能认证正确性，该阈值由模型错误的弥散程度决定；而奖励一致性的目标函数恰恰会破坏这种弥散性，导致准确率下降。这一发现对将一致性作为可靠性信号和自训练目标的做法提出了重要警示。

Abstract: A model's agreement across perturbed inputs is used both as a label-free reliability signal and as a self-training target, on the premise that agreement tracks correctness. That coupling is rarely measured directly: natural-image perturbations preserve meaning only by assumption, and no exact answer key localizes errors. Scientific figures remove both obstacles, a figure is drawn from data by a program, so redrawing it yields images that are semantically equivalent by construction and share a programmatically exact answer. We build RENDEQ, a generator of such render-equivalence sets, and measure the coupling on three open-weight VLMs, checking every finding across three independent instantiations. Re-rendering beats resampling on both accuracy and reliability. Agreement beats an evidence-carrying baseline, mean token log-probability, on two of three models and ties on the third, reversing an intermediate, buggy replication traced to a rendering-pipeline failure. The dispersion behind this is concentrated in one style factor, the plotting library, more than double the next-largest factor and an order of magnitude above the noise floor. Fine-tuning on the model's own cross-render consensus inverts: accuracy falls in every one of five replication runs, the opposite sign to published results on natural images. Agreement certifies correctness only above a threshold set by how diffuse a model's errors are, and an objective that rewards agreement destroys exactly that diffuseness.

</details>


### [244] [Consistency Has a Computable Blind Spot: A Commutation Theory of Label-Free Reliability for Vision-Language Figure Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05675)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于等变性的无标签视觉语言模型可靠性检测方法，证明不变性方法存在系统性盲区（误差与编辑可交换时无法检测），互补的等变性关系可以检测此类误差，并实例化为Equivariance-Consistency Score检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无标签可靠性检测依赖不变性原理（输入扰动后答案不变），但该原理存在根本性盲区：某些系统性误读在扰动后仍然存活，导致错误被错误地认证为正确。作者旨在揭示这一盲区的计算本质，并提出互补的等变性方法来弥补。

Method: 理论层面，证明"误差对编辑不可见"等价于"两者可交换"，从而不可达误差集合构成联合中心化子；互补地利用等变性——编辑数据后正确答案必须按可计算量改变。提出两对匹配编辑对仿射读取误差完备，循环重标记可缩小标签置换的差距。实例化为Equivariance-Consistency Score（无标签、免训练检测器），并发布REND-EQUIV数据集。

Result: 预测排序在三个模型和人工标注人群上得到验证；第二种不变性家族方法确认盲区属于关系本身而非实现；循环重标记在匹配的真实样本上带来预测的增益。理论同时解释了分类器蜕变测试文献中观测到的排序反转现象。

Conclusion: 可检测性是关系与故障类别的联合属性，而非关系单独决定。等变性补充不变性构成更完整的无标签可靠性检测框架，这一形式化表征统一了视觉语言模型与分类器蜕变测试两个领域的发现。

Abstract: Label-free reliability for vision-language models rests on invariance: perturb the input and a faithful reader's answer should not change. This has a known blind spot, a systematic misreading survives the perturbation and gets certified wrong, which we show is computable, not just real: an error is invisible to an edit exactly when the two commute, so the errors a suite cannot reach form its joint centralizer, a set that shrinks as edits are added and can be written down rather than guessed at. We act on the complementary relation, equivariance: edit a figure's data and the correct answer must change by a computable amount. Two matched edits are provably complete for affine reading errors; no suite of swap edits is complete for label permutations, and cyclic relabeling closes most of that gap. We instantiate the theory as the Equivariance-Consistency Score, a label-free, training-free detector, and release REND-EQUIV, pairing matched invariance and equivariance sets over identical data. The predicted ordering holds across three models and a hand-labeled population immune to the one circularity in how it is selected; a second invariance-family method confirms the blind spot belongs to the relation, not to any implementation; and cyclic relabeling delivers its predicted gain on a matched real sample. The same characterization explains a reported inversion of this ordering in the classifier metamorphic-testing literature: detectability is a joint property of the relation and the fault class, never of the relation alone.

</details>


### [245] [SEAM: Global consistency beyond local accuracy in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05702)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SEAM（科学解释可容许性机器），一个生成器无关的框架，用于解决科学机器学习中局部验证无法回答的"局部解释能否组装成全局可容许解释"的问题。SEAM通过有限解释层（SEAM-Ω）将每个区域表示为具有状态、闭包和观测通道的结构化解释，在重叠区域比较相邻解释并将分歧转化为通道解析的障碍（obstruction），从而定位不一致性并测试竞争性解释的合法性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学机器学习验证通常停留在局部层面（子域、基准划分或单次预测的解释），但无法回答这些局部解释能否整合为一个全局一致的科学解释。这种局部到全局的一致性问题目前缺乏可计算的方法。

Method: SEAM-Ω框架：将每个区域表示为含有状态、闭包和观测通道的结构化解释（可选合同元数据）；在邻居重叠处比较解释；将分歧转化为通道解析的障碍来定位不一致性；通过限制每个修复到某一声明所允许的修订范围来测试竞争性解释；精确修复不可用时，采用残差感知的正则化记录提供单独标注的经验归因。该框架还区分不一致性与不可识别性，并监控学习生成器在分布偏移下的行为。

Result: 建立了最小成本干预和守恒合同可检测性定理，以及可识别性和闭包可恢复性的配套结果。在19个实验（包括合成偏微分方程系统和分布外傅里叶神经算子（FNO）监控）中，SEAM即使在局部预测准确时也能检测到不兼容的解释，并将失败归因于特定的通道和重叠区域。

Conclusion: SEAM为现有求解器和学习模型增加了全局解释一致性审计能力，能够检验其局部解释是否构成连贯的科学解释，弥补了局部验证到全局可容许性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning commonly validates models at the level of a subdomain, a benchmark split, or an explanation for one prediction. Yet such local checks cannot establish whether the resulting explanations can be assembled into one globally admissible explanation. We introduce Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines (SEAM), a generator-agnostic framework that makes this local-to-global consistency question computable across regions, sensors, regimes, and model components. The finite explanation-sheaf instantiation SEAM-$Ω$ represents each region by a structured explanation with state, closure, and observation channels together with optional contract metadata; compares neighboring explanations on their overlaps; and converts disagreement into a channel-resolved obstruction. This obstruction locates inconsistency and tests competing declared accounts by restricting each repair to the revisions that one account permits. Exact feasibility refutes or retains an account; when exact repair is unavailable, residual-aware regularized records provide a separately labeled empirical attribution. The framework also separates inconsistency from non-identifiability and monitors learned generators under distribution shift. We establish theorems for minimum-cost intervention and conservation-contract detectability, together with companion results for identifiability and closure recoverability. Across nineteen experiments involving synthetic partial differential equation systems and out-of-distribution Fourier neural operator (FNO) monitoring, SEAM detects incompatible explanations even when local predictions are accurate, and attributes failures to specific channels and overlaps. SEAM adds a global explanation-consistency audit to existing solvers and learning models, testing whether their local explanations form a coherent scientific account.

</details>


### [246] [Spectral Aliasing Pretext: A novel task for Self-Supervised fault diagnosis in rotating machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05705)
*Victor Gialis,Maxime Metz,David Esteve,Abdenour Soualhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频谱混叠的自监督学习方法SAP，通过故意欠采样振动数据以产生折叠频谱，并训练Transformer重建原始频谱，从而在无标签数据上预训练模型。在CWRU数据集上，SAP结合线性探测在少量标注数据下即可获得高分类性能和低方差，整体效果优于全监督训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在机械故障诊断中表现出色，但严重依赖大量标注数据，而工业场景中标注数据稀缺且获取成本高昂。现有方法难以在有限标注条件下有效训练模型。

Method: 提出SAP（频谱混叠预文本任务）自监督学习方法：故意对振动信号进行欠采样以产生折叠频谱，训练Transformer模型从折叠频谱中重建原始未折叠频谱。该预文本任务迫使模型学习与机械故障相关的频域不变特征，同时避免使用可能破坏性的数据增强手段。

Result: 在CWRU数据集上，SAP学习到的表征具有稳定性和高判别性。在线性探测设置下，仅需少量标注数据即可快速达到很高的分类性能，且方差低。相比之下，全微调（包括全监督训练）并未带来更稳定或更好的结果。

Conclusion: SAP结合线性探测在有限标注数据的故障诊断场景下，比全监督训练更有效、更可靠，为解决工业标注数据稀缺问题提供了新的自监督学习路径。

Abstract: Deep learning is a new way for machinery fault diagnosis but requires extensive labeled data, a scarce resource in industrial settings. We propose Spectral Aliasing Pretext (SAP), a self-supervised learning method that pretrains models on unlabeled vibration data by exploiting spectral aliasing. We deliberately undersample signals to create folded spectrum, then train a Transformer to reconstruct the original unfolded spectrum. This pretext task forces the model to learn frequency-domain invariants characteristic of mechanical faults, without potentially destructive augmentations. Experiments on the CWRU dataset show that SAP learns stable and highly discriminative representations. In a linear probing setting, SAP quickly achieves very high classification performance with only a small fraction of labeled data and low variance. In contrast, full fine-tuning, including fully supervised training, does not lead to more stable or better results. Overall, these findings suggest that SAP combined with linear probing can be more effective and reliable than fully supervised training for fault diagnosis with limited labeled data.

</details>


### [247] [CircuitSteer: Geometrically Aligned Multi-Layer Steering via Sparse Autoencoder Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05732)
*Mehrshad Saadatinia,Parsa Razmara,Ardalan Aryashad,Ali Abbasi,Seyedarmin Azizi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CircuitSteer框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）识别并操控跨多层的语义电路，通过特征共激活和几何对齐构建特征流电路，合成稠密引导向量并进行多点干预，在毒性、情感强度、谄媚和拒答等多个任务上实现了比静态单点干预方法更稳健有效的行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM行为引导方法（如CAA）依赖从聚合激活差异中得到的固定单层干预向量，对所有语义多样的输入施加单一干预，且难以在跨层维持一致的行为改变，限制了引导的有效性和鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 使用SAE识别跨多个层的连贯语义电路；2) 基于特征共激活和解码器方向的几何对齐构建特征流电路，隔离负责目标行为的多层子电路；3) 从稀疏特征合成稠密引导向量；4) 应用多点干预来引导模型内部语义轨迹。

Result: 在毒性、情感强度、谄媚和拒答等多个任务及两个模型家族上进行评估，CircuitSteer是唯一在所有模型和数据集上始终产生保持流畅性干预的方法；对比方法要么牺牲文本质量，要么覆盖不足，在谄媚和拒答等复杂行为上完全失败。

Conclusion: 通过强制所选特征间的几何对齐实现的多层电路引导，比静态单点干预产生严格更稳健和有效的行为控制。

Abstract: Controlling the behavior of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge for AI alignment. Existing steering methods, such as Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), typically rely on fixed single-layer interventions derived from aggregate activation differences. These methods impose a single intervention across semantically diverse inputs and often fail to sustain consistent behavioral changes across layers, limiting the effectiveness of the steering. In this work, we introduce CircuitSteer, a novel framework that leverages Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to identify and manipulate coherent semantic circuits distributed across multiple layers. By constructing a feature flow circuit based on feature co-activation and the geometric alignment of decoder directions, we isolate the specific multi-layer subcircuits responsible for a target behavior. We then synthesize dense steering vectors from these sparse features and apply multi-point interventions to guide the model's internal semantic trajectory. We evaluate CircuitSteer using contrastive examples across a diverse set of tasks, including toxicity, emotion-intensity, sycophancy, and refusal, spanning two model families. Across all models and datasets, CircuitSteer is the only method to consistently produce fluency-preserving interventions; competing methods either sacrifice text quality or lack coverage, failing entirely on complex behaviors like sycophancy and refusal. These results demonstrate that multi-layer circuit steering, enabled by enforcing geometric alignment among selected features, yields strictly more robust and effective behavioral control than static single-point interventions. Code is available at https://github.com/mehrshad-sdtn/CircuitSteer.

</details>


### [248] [Multivariate Time Series Forecasting needs Cross Variable Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05742)
*Kuiye Ding,Yifan Hu,Hanchen Wang,Hao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CvLoss（跨变量损失），一种即插即用的结构正则化器，通过对预测残差在跨变量图上施加约束，解决多变量时间序列预测中直接预测（DF）范式与跨变量及滞后依赖不匹配的目标差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型主要关注历史观测中的跨变量依赖，却忽略了未来值之间的相互依赖关系；直接预测（DF）范式使用逐点目标函数，未显式约束跨变量结构，导致目标函数与真实数据依赖模式不匹配。

Method: 提出CvLoss，一种即插即用的结构正则化器，在跨变量图上约束预测残差。它通过对预测patch上的边级残差差异进行惩罚，鼓励同步和异步交互的一致性。

Result: 实验表明CvLoss能够持续提升多种竞争性预测模型的性能，优于代表性学习目标，并且兼容多种预测骨干网络。

Conclusion: CvLoss通过引入跨变量结构约束有效弥合了DF目标的差距，为多变量时间序列预测提供了通用且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting presents unique challenges because future variables often co-evolve under shared system dynamics. While existing studies mainly focus on cross-variable dependencies in historical observations, dependencies among future values are much less explored. Specifically, modern forecasting models largely follow the Direct Forecasting (DF) paradigm, generating multi-step forecasts with point-wise objectives that do not explicitly constrain cross-variable structure. In this work, we show that the DF objective is mismatched in the presence of cross-variable and lagged dependencies, revealing an objective gap. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{C}ross-\textbf{V}ariable \textbf{Loss} (CvLoss), a plug-in structural regularizer that constrains forecast residuals on a cross-variable graph. CvLoss penalizes inconsistent edge-wise residual differences over forecast patches, encouraging consistency across both synchronous and asynchronous interactions. Our experiments show that CvLoss consistently improves competitive forecasting models, outperforms representative learning objectives, and is compatible with a variety of forecasting backbones.

</details>


### [249] [Equipment-centric workpiece localization in near real-time using deep learning-based vision and event-driven finite state machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05744)
*Dohyeon Kong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种以设备为中心的热锻造工件连续定位框架，通过多台静态2D摄像头观测处理设备，结合关键点引导注意力机制的3D CNN识别抓取和释放活动，并使用事件驱动有限状态机持续更新工件状态与位置。在实际热锻造工厂中实现了100%事件检测准确率（33秒容差窗口内）、317.8mm的平均定位误差和21秒的平均系统延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热锻造过程中工件直接追踪因极端温度、表面退化和不规则路径而不可靠，需要一种替代方案来实现工件的连续定位，以满足可追溯性和工艺协调的需求。

Method: 采用设备为中心的框架：多台静态2D相机观测处理设备，在楼面平面空间中估计3D设备坐标并识别抓取/释放活动；使用事件驱动有限状态机验证活动并持续更新工件状态与位置；在3D卷积神经网络中集成关键点引导的注意力机制以增强活动识别。

Result: 在实际热锻造工厂评估中：事件检测准确率100%（33秒容差窗口内），平均定位误差317.8毫米，平均系统延迟21秒。

Conclusion: 该框架将基于视觉的感知与可解释的事件驱动推理相结合，支持工件转移可视化和设备操作的定量分析，为热锻造中的工件定位提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Continuous workpiece localization is essential for traceability and process coordination in hot forging, but direct tracking is unreliable because of extreme temperatures, surface degradation, and irregular routing. This study presents an equipment-centric framework that infers workpiece locations from handling equipment observed by multiple static 2D cameras. The framework estimates floorplan-space 3D equipment coordinates and recognizes grasp and release activities. Event-driven finite state machines validate these activities as discrete handling events and continuously update workpiece states and locations. A keypoint-guided attention mechanism integrated into a 3D convolutional neural network improves activity recognition by focusing on functionally relevant equipment regions. Evaluation in an operational hot forging factory achieved 100\% event detection accuracy within a 33-second tolerance window, a mean localization error of 317.8 mm, and a mean system latency of 21 seconds. The framework connects vision-based perception with interpretable event-driven reasoning and supports visualization of workpiece transfers and quantitative analysis of equipment operations.

</details>


### [250] [Accelerating nanodrug development in continuous flow systems using informed prediction models based on low-cost surrogate nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05761)
*Kai Dahms,Eilien Heinrich,Jochen Schmid,Michael Bortz,Iryna Savych,Regina Bleul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出并验证了一种基于形状约束的预测建模方法，用于在微流控条件下预测脂质体和脂质纳米颗粒的粒径及多分散指数，从而减少纳米药物开发中对大量实验筛选的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米治疗药物的开发通常依赖大量经验性优化，因为纳米颗粒的性质（如粒径和多分散指数）对工艺参数（如浓度、流速和混合比）非常敏感。缺乏预测性数学模型导致必须进行迭代实验筛选，增加了成本和开发时间。

Method: 采用微流控方法在受控条件下制备脂质体和脂质纳米颗粒，系统改变脂质浓度、流速和水相-有机相混合比。构建基于形状约束的预测模型，结合实验数据和专家知识进行训练，并在仅使用最少实验数据的情况下对制药应用进行模型验证。

Result: 形状约束模型能够准确预测纳米颗粒的粒径和分散性，减少了开展大量实验工作的需求，支持高效理性的纳米药物工艺开发。

Conclusion: 形状约束建模为纳米医学制造中的工艺开发提供了理性且高效的预测框架，有助于降低开发成本并缩短研发周期。

Abstract: The development of nanotherapeutics often involves extensive empirical optimization due to the sensitivity of nanoparticle properties, such as size and polydispersity index (PDI), to minor changes in process parameters. Factors like formulation concentration, flow rates, and mixing ratios can significantly influence clinical efficacy and therapeutic outcomes. The absence of predictive mathematical frameworks has made iterative experimental screening necessary, increasing both costs and development time.
  This study introduces and validates a predictive modeling approach based on shape constraints, aiming to enhance the estimation of nanoparticle characteristics across various process conditions. Using controlled microfluidic methods, liposomes and lipid nanoparticles were systematically prepared under varying lipid concentrations, flow rates, and aqueous-to-organic mixing ratios.
  The shape-constrained model, informed by both experimental data and expert knowledge, was subsequently validated for a pharmaceutical application using minimal empirical data. Results reveal that shape-constrained modeling facilitates accurate prediction of nanoparticle size and dispersity, reducing the need for extensive experimental workflows. This framework supports rational and efficient process development for manufacturing nanomedicine systems.

</details>


### [251] [Neuro-Symbolic Closed-Loop Control of Laser Powder Bed Fusion with an In-Loop Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05773)
*Gisuk Hong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何条件约束的神经符号闭环架构用于激光粉末床熔融（LPBF）工艺，将标准对齐的本体论嵌入控制回路中，结合符号推理与统计学习来设定约束感知预测控制器的目标。该架构通过描述逻辑推理器将工艺目标与约束映射为每个扫描路径的参考值和边界，并通过高斯过程提供几何相关和功率相关的熔池深度-宽度比及其校准不确定性。在Eagar-Tsai代理模型（基于NIST AM-Bench基准校准的IN625）上验证，该架构消除了几何盲控制器产生的挂渣（dross）缺陷，且在双重评分下仅存少量残余的未熔合缺陷，在故意模型失配下能优雅降级，并可通过编辑本体数据而非代码来适配新合金和约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光粉末床熔融工艺中，熔池深度等关键质量指标无法在构建过程中直接测量，限制了闭环质量控制。现有方法缺乏将工艺目标、约束与可控信号系统化关联的机制，需要一种能融合符号推理与统计学习的架构来解决这种不可观测质量约束的控制问题。

Method: 提出几何条件约束的神经符号闭环架构：1）标准对齐的本体论嵌入控制回路，连接工艺目标/约束与可观测信号；2）描述逻辑推理器将约束转换为每条扫描路径的参考值和边界；3）高斯过程提供几何和功率依赖的深度-宽度比及其校准不确定性；4）推理器分类几何特征并选择激活约束（如过悬处的缺熔下限、单调保护、能量密度上限）；5）仅基于几何上下文变化时运行小二次规划问题实现每路径控制。

Result: 在Eagar-Tsai代理模型（校准至NIST AM-Bench IN625基准）上：消除了几何盲控制器产生的挂渣缺陷；双重评分下保持零挂渣仅存少量残余缺熔；在故意模型失配下性能优雅降级；通过编辑本体数据而非重写代码即可重新适配新合金和约束。

Conclusion: 该架构验证了神经符号方法在LPBF闭环控制中的可行性，下一步主要工作是实验校准深度-宽度比模型。本体数据驱动的可编辑性为工艺适应不同合金和约束条件提供了灵活途径。

Abstract: A geometry-conditioned, neuro-symbolic closed-loop architecture is proposed for laser powder bed fusion, in which a standards-aligned ontology operates inside the control loop and couples symbolic reasoning with statistical learning to set the targets of a constraint-aware predictive controller. The ontology links the process objectives and constraints to the signals a controller can observe, and a description-logic reasoner converts them into the references and bounds enforced on each scan. The demonstrated case is overhang dross, a quality limit on the melt pool depth, which governs quality yet cannot be measured during the build, is mapped through a geometry- and power-dependent depth-to-width ratio onto a bound on the observable width, with the ratio and its calibrated uncertainty supplied by a Gaussian process. The reasoner classifies each upcoming feature and selects the active constraints-adding a lack-of-fusion floor at overhangs, a monotone guard beyond the calibrated range, and an energy-density cap where a process window is declared while running only on changes of geometric context and otherwise leaving a single small quadratic program on the per-scan path. In an Eagar-Tsai surrogate calibrated to the NIST AM-Bench benchmark for IN625, the architecture eliminates the dross produced by a geometry-blind controller, holds dross at zero with only a small residual lack-of-fusion under dual scoring, degrades gracefully under deliberate plant mismatch, and retargets to new alloys and constraints by editing ontology data rather than code. The results establish architectural feasibility, experimental calibration of the ratio is the principal next step.

</details>


### [252] [GROM: Gradient-Free Rapid One-Shot Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05783)
*Paweł Batorski,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GROM，一种基于封闭式解析解的单次机器遗忘方法，通过岭回归正则化最小二乘优化直接计算权重更新，无需迭代微调，在保持模型性能的同时高效移除特定知识，并能抵御低比特量化攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于微调的机器遗忘方法虽然广泛使用，但存在计算成本高、缺乏解析形式、目标知识仅被隐藏而非真正删除等问题——简单量化操作即可恢复大部分已遗忘内容，需要一种更高效且更彻底的遗忘方案。

Method: 将遗忘过程建模为岭正则化最小二乘优化问题，推导出针对权重矩阵的封闭式加性更新公式。该更新强制选定层抑制不需要的内容，同时严格保持其对保留数据的行为。整个计算仅依赖无梯度的前向传播，无需反向传播和迭代收敛，可在数秒内完成权重编辑。

Result: 在TOFU-5%、TOFU-10%、MUSE-Books、MUSE-News和WMDP基准上，GROM取得了最先进的遗忘-效用权衡结果，计算开销比传统微调低数个数量级，且能抵御低比特量化攻击（该攻击可恢复基于梯度基线方法本应遗忘的内容）。

Conclusion: GROM通过封闭式解析解实现了高效、稳健的机器遗忘，不仅大幅降低了计算成本，还从根本上解决了"内容被隐藏而非删除"的问题，为LLM知识移除提供了一种更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning has become a critical capability for safely removing specific, sensitive knowledge from large language models (LLMs). Current state-of-the-art approaches primarily rely on iterative, training-time unlearning via fine-tuning. However, even when utilizing parameter-efficient dimensionality reduction techniques like LoRA, gradient-based optimization remains computationally expensive and lacks explicit analytical formulations. It can also leave the targeted knowledge merely hidden rather than removed, to the point that simply quantizing the unlearned model restores much of what it was supposed to have erased. To resolve this, we propose a novel one-shot unlearning approach, abandoning iterative optimization in favor of a direct, exact analytical solution. We frame the unlearning process as a ridge-regularized least-squares optimization problem, deriving a closed-form additive update for targeted weight matrices. This update forces the selected layer to suppress unwanted content while strictly preserving its behavior on retained data. Computed from gradient-free forward passes alone, with no backpropagation and no iteration to convergence, GROM applies the weight edit in mere seconds, which makes it orders of magnitude faster than traditional fine-tuning. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that GROM achieves state-of-the-art forgetting-utility trade-offs on TOFU-5%, TOFU-10%, MUSE-Books, MUSE-News and WMDP, significantly reducing computational overhead without sacrificing overall model performance. Because the update removes the targeted content from the weights instead of masking it, GROM also withstands the low-bit quantization attack that recovers much of the content a gradient-based baseline had appeared to forget. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/GROM.

</details>


### [253] [Predicting Task Difficulty Without Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05797)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在17个agentic基准测试中进行事前（ex ante）任务难度预测的问题，发现AUC指标会掩盖差的难度估计，识别出token级熵作为有效的预测信号，并展示了期望难度与实际难度之间的残差可以揭示环境缺陷如数据污染和不可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长时域智能体任务中，经验性的试错方法计算成本极高，而通过任务描述事先预测难度可以校准评估基准、构建渐进式训练课程，但先前工作局限于静态任务或隔离的编码环境，依赖窄特征和不准确的评估指标。

Method: 跨17个agentic基准（涵盖编码、数学、机器学习、网页导航、函数调用等领域）进行事前难度预测研究，分析AUC指标的局限性，探索token级熵作为预测信号，并通过期望与观测难度的残差分析来暴露环境隐藏缺陷。

Result: 发现AUC会掩盖差的难度估计；token级熵被识别为有用的预测特征；残差分析能够揭示如数据污染和不可行性等环境缺陷。

Conclusion: 事前难度预测在长时域agentic领域具有重要意义，现有评估指标（AUC）不够可靠，需要更有效的预测信号和残差分析来提升预测质量并诊断环境问题。

Abstract: Task difficulty dictates an agent's likelihood of success, and estimating it without rollouts means forecasting this directly from a task description before executing costly simulations in stateful environments. Reliable estimates would therefore allow environment designers to calibrate evaluation benchmarks and construct progressive training curricula. This becomes increasingly important as agents move into long-horizon domains, where empirical trial-and-error is a severe computational bottleneck. Prior work on early prediction is limited to static tasks or isolated coding environments, often relying on narrow features and inaccurate evaluation metrics. We study \textit{ex ante} difficulty prediction across 17 agentic benchmarks spanning coding, mathematics, machine learning, web navigation, function calling, and other domains. We show that AUC can mask poor difficulty estimates, identify token-level entropy as a useful predictive signal, and show how residuals between expected and observed difficulty can expose hidden environment flaws such as contamination and infeasibility.

</details>


### [254] [Learning to Rank Tensor Network Contraction Plans for GPU-Accelerated Quantum Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05819)
*Alfred M. Pastor,Maribel Castillo,Jose M. Badia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个学习排序（Learning-to-Rank）框架，用于在GPU上执行前选择高效的张量网络收缩计划。通过从成对收缩序列中提取结构特征，并用梯度提升排序器（基于列表级和成对级目标）从GPU测量数据中训练模型，在多种电路族上评估后发现，学习的排序器通常能找到更优的收缩计划，其中列表级模型决策质量最佳。跨GPU架构的排序有一定稳定性但非完全保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络收缩可以降低量子电路经典模拟的计算成本，但收缩计划的选择对GPU上的实际执行效率影响巨大。具有相似理论复杂度的计划在GPU上可能因为并行性、归约结构、内存流量和收缩几何等因素而表现迥异，单纯依赖理论复杂度或启发式（如MinFill）难以选出最优计划，因此需要一种数据驱动的方法来预训练预测收缩计划的性能。

Method: 提出一个学习排序框架：1）每个收缩计划由其成对收缩序列中直接提取的结构特征表示；2）使用梯度提升排序器，基于列表级（listwise）和成对级（pairwise）目标函数，从GPU实际测量数据中训练模型；3）在分布内测试集和电路族迁移测试集上评估，并与随机基线和MinFill基线比较；4）额外研究后端迁移，即在不同GPU架构上评估模型排序的稳定性。

Result: 学习的排序器通常能识别出优于随机和MinFill基线的收缩计划，其中列表级模型的整体决策质量最强。在跨GPU架构迁移实验中，排名在GPU间保持较大程度的稳定性（尽管不完全一致），且源设备训练的模型在第二台设备上无需重新训练仍能保留有用的决策质量。

Conclusion: Learning to Rank是减少收缩计划搜索空间的一种实用方法，能够有效预测GPU上张量网络收缩计划的相对性能。然而，性能仍部分依赖后端硬件，说明在跨架构部署时需要谨慎对待模型的迁移能力。

Abstract: Classical simulation remains essential for developing and validating quantum algorithms, but its cost grows rapidly with circuit size. Tensor-network contraction can reduce this cost by exploiting circuit structure, although its efficiency depends strongly on the chosen contraction plan. On GPUs, plans with similar theoretical complexity may perform very differently because execution also depends on parallelism, reduction structure, memory traffic, and contraction geometry. We present a learning-to-rank framework for selecting efficient contraction plans before executing them. Each plan is represented by structural features derived directly from its sequence of pairwise contractions, and gradient-boosted rankers are trained from GPU measurements using listwise and pairwise objectives. We evaluate the resulting models on diverse circuit families, using separate in-distribution and circuit-family-shift test sets, and compare them with random and MinFill-based baselines. The learned rankers generally identify better plans, with the listwise model providing the strongest overall decision quality. We also study backend shift by comparing empirical plan orderings on two GPU architectures and evaluating the source-trained models on the second device without retraining. The rankings remain substantially, though not perfectly, stable across GPUs, and the models retain useful decision quality. These results support Learning to Rank as a practical way to reduce contraction-plan search, while showing that performance remains partly backend dependent.

</details>


### [255] [Evidential Rule Learning for Interpretable Classification with Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05859)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FERL（快速证据规则学习），一种可解释的模糊规则学习方法，其输出具有证据性质（belief、plausibility和abstention能力），无需辅助头、留出集或重复推理即可直接获得，且在准确性、集合预测和OOD检测方面均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释分类在实际部署中不仅需要准确预测，还需要模型对决策依据透明，并在无法可靠决策时选择弃权。现有的后处理校准方法无法使模型的证据能力与学习过程本身深度融合。

Method: FERL通过单次确定性前向传播，直接从模糊隶属度中产生证据输出（belief、plausibility和abstention），无需额外模块。理论分析证明了FERL具有Lipschitz稳定性，保证证据输出随输入平滑变化。

Result: 在30个表格数据集基准上，FERL比最强规则学习器平均准确率高2.6%；集合预测在u65/u80上达到0.80/0.83（优于朴素credal分类器的0.79/0.80），集合覆盖率0.92 vs ≤0.82；在表格近OOD检测上AUROC 77.7 vs 77.4；在CUB和AwA2概念瓶颈评估中与最强专用检测器差距在2.3 AUROC以内，并在AwA2上取得最佳AUPR-Out（68.3）和新类拒绝（57.2）性能，且能指出异常属性。

Conclusion: FERL将证据推理与模糊规则学习统一在一个框架中，同时实现了可解释性、准确性和可靠的置信度评估，在多个任务上均达到或超越专用方法的性能。

Abstract: Interpretable classification often requires more than accurate predictions for real-life deployment: models should be transparent about the evidence behind their decisions and abstain when they cannot decide reliably. We introduce Fast Evidential Rule Learning (FERL), a method that learns interpretable, accurate fuzzy rule models whose outputs are evidential. Unlike post-hoc calibration, FERL's belief, plausibility, and abstention capabilities arise directly from the fuzzy memberships in a single deterministic pass, with no auxiliary head, held-out set, or repeated inference. Our theoretical analysis further shows that FERL is Lipschitz stable, which means that its evidential outputs vary smoothly with the input. Against state-of-the-art rule learners, FERL is statistically significantly more accurate across a 30 tabular-dataset benchmark ($+2.6\%$ average accuracy over the second best). Its native set predictions attain the best utility-discounted accuracy among credal classifiers ($u_{65}/u_{80}=0.80/0.83$ vs.\ $0.79/0.80$ for the naive credal classifier), at higher set coverage ($0.92$ vs.\ $\le0.82$). FERL also matches dedicated out-of-distribution detectors on tabular near-OOD detection ($77.7$ vs.\ $77.4$ AUROC for the strongest baseline). Under detector-class-disjoint concept-bottleneck evaluation, its it is within $2.3$ AUROC points of the strongest dedicated detector on both CUB and AwA2, while attaining the best AwA2 AUPR-Out ($68.3$) and novel-class rejection ($57.2$), while being able to name which attributes are anomalous.

</details>


### [256] [Beyond Feature Importance: A Comparative Analysis of Pattern Detection Methods in Cluster Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05880)
*Benjamin Connor,Anna Jurek-Loughrey,Lu Bai,Muhammad Fahim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对聚类结果解释中的模式检测方法进行了比较评估，通过引入注入预定义模式的合成数据集，评估了随机森林代理模型、LIME和主成分分析三种常用后验分析方法，发现它们虽能恢复相关特征，但均无法一致地检测所有注入的模式类型，揭示了现有可解释性工具与模式级聚类解释需求之间的关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类结果的解释是数据分析中的根本挑战，尤其在医疗保健等高维数据领域。现有可解释性技术主要针对特征重要性或局部实例级解释，而非识别聚类中存在的结构模式，缺乏对模式检测方法的系统比较评估。

Method: 构建了一套注入预定义模式的合成数据集用于受控评估，比较了三种广泛使用的后验分析方法：基于随机森林代理模型的置换特征重要性、LIME（局部可解释模型无关解释）和主成分分析。

Result: 三种方法虽然都能成功恢复相关特征，但没有一种方法能一致地检测所有注入的模式类型，说明现有方法在模式级聚类解释方面存在明显局限。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了现有可解释性工具与模式级聚类解释需求之间的关键差距，为开发专门的模式检测方法提供了动机和方向。

Abstract: Interpreting clustering outcomes remains a fundamental challenge in data analysis, particularly in domains such as healthcare where meaningful patterns must be extracted from high-dimensional data. While numerous explainability techniques exist, they are primarily designed to assess feature importance or provide local instance-level explanations rather than to identify structured patterns present within clusters. This work presents a comparative evaluation of commonly used post-hoc analysis methods for pattern detection in clustering results. To enable controlled evaluation, we introduce a suite of synthetic datasets in which predefined patterns are systematically injected. Three widely used techniques are evaluated: a Random Forest surrogate model with permutation feature importance, LIME (Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations), and principal component analysis. Results demonstrate that although each method can successfully recover relevant features, none consistently detects all injected pattern types. These findings high- light a critical gap between existing explainability tools and the requirements of pattern-level cluster interpretation, motivating the development of dedicated pattern detection methodologies.

</details>


### [257] [Alternating Levenberg-Marquardt Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Fourier-Enhanced Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05892)
*Yulun Wu,Matthieu Barreau,Miguel Aguiar,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FALM-PINN优化框架，通过上下层分离表示学习与系数拟合，使用傅里叶增强基和Levenberg-Marquardt算法解决PINN在高频和多尺度PDE上的谱偏差与表示-系数耦合问题，误差比现有最优基线低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在处理高频、多尺度解及强非线性PDE时常常失败，根本原因在于两个因素：一是谱偏差导致神经网络欠拟合高频特征；二是表示学习与系数拟合在单一非凸优化目标中相互纠缠（表示-系数耦合）。这两个问题严重限制了PINN在复杂科学计算问题中的应用。

Method: FALM-PINN采用双层优化框架：上层问题学习傅里叶增强基函数，丰富潜空间中的高频成分；下层问题在该基上拟合投影系数，并使用Levenberg-Marquardt算法求解非线性最小二乘问题，从而解耦表示学习与系数拟合。对于线性PDE，框架退化为单步凸优化问题。作者证明了两种情况下交替训练方案的全局收敛性。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的高频和非线性PDE数值实验中，FALM-PINN的相对L2误差比最先进的基线方法低最多两个数量级，效果显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FALM-PINN通过解耦表示学习与系数拟合，有效克服了PINN的谱偏差和表示-系数耦合问题，适用于一般非线性和耦合PDE系统，并在理论收敛性和实际精度方面均展现出优越性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often fail to accurately resolve partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency or multi-scale solutions, as well as strongly nonlinear problems. Two factors underlie this difficulty: spectral bias, the tendency of neural networks to underfit high-frequency features; and representation-coefficient coupling, the entanglement of representation learning and coefficient fitting within a single nonconvex optimization objective. In this work, we propose the Fourier-enhanced alternating Levenberg--Marquardt PINN (FALM-PINN), an optimization framework that decouples representation learning from coefficient fitting. The upper-level problem learns a Fourier-enhanced basis that enriches the latent space with high-frequency components, while the lower-level problem resolves the coupling by fitting the projection coefficients on this basis, solving a nonlinear least-squares problem with the Levenberg--Marquardt algorithm. The framework applies to general nonlinear and coupled PDE systems, and reduces to a single-step convex optimization problem for linear PDEs. We prove global convergence of the alternating training scheme in both cases. Numerical examples on multiple challenging high-frequency and nonlinear PDEs show that FALM-PINN achieves relative $L^2$ errors up to two orders of magnitude lower than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [258] [CohortHijack: Robustness of Single Cell Annotation to Companion Cell Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05900)
*Arash Vashagh,Yasmin Vashagh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CohortHijack，一种针对单细胞注释中邻域精细化步骤的鲁棒性审计方法，通过移除查询队列中的非目标细胞来改变最终注释标签，同时保持目标细胞的表达谱、基础预测和训练模型不变。实验表明结构化移除比随机移除更有效，多起点搜索可改变约24%的线性SVM目标和约20%的逻辑回归目标，且副作用很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单细胞注释工具通常会利用邻近细胞或聚类投票来精细化初始细胞标签，但这种方法是否可以被恶意操纵——即在不改变目标细胞本身的情况下，通过调整其周围细胞来改变最终标签——尚未被系统研究。作者旨在揭示这一潜在的攻击面。

Method: 提出 CohortHijack 鲁棒性审计框架，移除查询队列中的选定非目标细胞，同时保留目标表达谱、基础预测和训练模型。比较随机与结构化移除策略，并采用贪心搜索、多起点搜索和束搜索三种搜索方法。使用逻辑回归和校准线性SVM分类器，在PBMC3K和Paul15数据集上评估，并使用CellTypist多数投票进行额外验证。

Result: 在Paul15上，结构化移除一致优于随机移除。多起点搜索改变了24.33%的线性SVM目标和19.67%的逻辑回归目标，仅移除少量队列细胞且平均附带变化低于0.4%。消融实验确认禁用邻域精细化后效果消失。CellTypist多数投票的独立预测未受影响，但其精细化标签在小量伴细胞移除后发生了变化。

Conclusion: 查询队列组成是单细胞注释中一个可被利用的"保持目标不变"的攻击面。邻域精细化过程对队列中伴细胞的移除高度敏感，这提示需要对该类注释方法进行更严格的鲁棒性考量。

Abstract: Many single-cell annotation tools refine an initial cell label using nearby cells or cluster-level voting. We study whether this refinement can be manipulated without changing the target cell. We introduce CohortHijack, a robustness audit that removes selected non-target cells from the query cohort while preserving the target expression profile, base prediction, and trained model. We evaluate random and structured removal methods, together with greedy, multi-start, and beam search, on PBMC3K and Paul15 using logistic regression and calibrated linear SVM classifiers. Structured removal was consistently stronger than random removal on Paul15. Multi-start search changed 24.33% of linear-SVM targets and 19.67% of logistic-regression targets while removing a small fraction of the cohort and keeping mean collateral changes below 0.4%. Ablations confirmed that the effect disappeared when neighborhood refinement was disabled. We also evaluated CellTypist majority voting, where independent predictions remained unchanged across all evaluations, but refined labels changed after small companion-cell removals. These findings identify query cohort composition as a target-preserving attack surface in single-cell annotation.

</details>


### [259] [BioM-JEPA: joint-embedding prediction of graph-connected gene blocks in single cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05928)
*Yuhao Wang,Zelin Zang,Yuxuan Liu,Zhen Lei,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioM-JEPA 是一种基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的单细胞转录组自监督学习方法，通过预测图连接的基因块表示而非逐基因重建，获得了更高质量的表征和更高的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞转录组数据是稀疏的，现有的自监督模型大多通过重建单个基因来学习表征，但这种方式忽略了基因之间的协调生物学程序。作者希望利用基因之间的关联信息（蛋白质关联和共表达证据）来构建更优的预测单元。

Method: BioM-JEPA 采用联合嵌入预测架构：将基因划分为由蛋白质关联和语料库共表达证据定义的图连接基因块；学生网络从细胞中其余基因推断每个目标块的表示，而缓慢更新的教师网络则从完整基因集提供对应目标；使用线性注意力避免构建二次规模的基因-基因注意力矩阵。

Result: 块级预测产生的嵌入在有效秩和基因深度关联方面的诊断优于标记预测、随机块和重建对照组；在CellBench任务中冻结嵌入保留了表达、通路和邻域信息，并在扰动响应误差上取得最优结果；在匹配的一轮hPancreas实验中，BioM-JEPA相较于scFoundation实现了5.75倍微调吞吐量和3.76倍嵌入吞吐量的提升。

Conclusion: 图连接的基因块是JEPA风格表征学习在单细胞生物学中的有效预测单元，兼顾了表征质量与计算效率。

Abstract: Single-cell transcriptomes are sparse observations of coordinated biological programmes, yet most self-supervised models learn by reconstructing individual genes. Here we present BioM-JEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architecture that instead predicts aggregate representations of graph-connected gene blocks defined by protein-association and corpus-derived coexpression evidence. A student network infers each target-block representation from the remaining genes in a cell, while a slowly updated teacher supplies the corresponding target from the full observed gene set. Under the reported extraction procedure, block-level prediction produced embeddings with higher effective rank and weaker association with detected-gene depth in the tested diagnostics than token-prediction, random-block and reconstruction controls. Across CellBench tasks, frozen BioM-JEPA embeddings retained expression, pathway and neighbourhood information and achieved the lowest aggregate perturbation-response error among the evaluated models. Representation diagnostics were also consistent with canonical pancreatic programmes and compositional relationships between genetic perturbations. Linear attention avoids constructing a quadratic gene-by-gene attention matrix; in a matched one-epoch hPancreas experiment at batch size 8, BioM-JEPA provided 5.75-fold higher fine-tuning throughput and 3.76-fold higher held-out embedding throughput than scFoundation. Together, these results support graph-connected gene blocks as useful prediction units for JEPA-style representation learning in single-cell biology.

</details>


### [260] [How Far Do Simple Transformations Translate Across Text Embedding Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05980)
*Sid Ali Hamideche,Louis Adrien Dufrene,Quentin Lampin,Guillaume Larue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了简单的线性映射能否在不同文本嵌入模型之间转换表示，测试了"潜在通用性"假设。在九个架构、池化策略和训练目标各异的嵌入模型上，发现简单翻译器对部分模型对有效，但对其他模型对效果较差，表明异构嵌入空间并非普遍通过简单映射相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证文献中关于"潜在通用性"的假设，即在无需解码为人类可读文本的情况下，实现AI到AI的潜在通信。作者希望在超越简化基准的现实文本设置中测试这一假设。

Method: 使用线性映射等轻量级翻译器，在九个不同的嵌入模型（在架构、池化策略和训练目标上有所差异）上进行实验，通过CKA、下游迁移、保真度和检索四个指标评估模型间的兼容性。

Result: 简单翻译器能恢复有意义的共享结构并支持部分兼容模型对的迁移，但对其他模型对效果显著失败。兼容性取决于架构、训练目标、池化和数据分布的联合影响。

Conclusion: 异构嵌入空间并非如一些文献所声称的那样通过简单映射普遍相关，简单变换仅对特定兼容模型对有效，潜在通用性假设在现实文本设置中得不到全面支持。

Abstract: We investigate whether simple transformations can translate representations across heterogeneous text embedding models. Understanding how independently trained models organize semantic information is an enabler for AI-to-AI latent communication without decoding into human-readable text. Focusing on lightweight translators such as linear mappings, we test the literature hypothesis of latent universality in a realistic text setting beyond simplified benchmarks. Across nine embedding models differing in architecture, pooling strategy, and training objective, we evaluate compatibility using CKA, downstream transfer, fidelity, and retrieval. Simple translators recover meaningful shared structure and support transfer for some compatible pairs, but fail sharply for others. Compatibility depends jointly on architecture, training objective, pooling, and data distribution. Overall, the results show that heterogeneous embedding spaces are not universally related by simple mappings as often suggested in some literature.

</details>


### [261] [THBKG: A Temporal Biomedical Knowledge Graph for Decision-Aligned Clinical Advancement Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05982)
*Pui Chung Siu,Claudia Cabrera,Mani Mudaliar,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了时间异构生物医学知识图谱（THBKG），用于描述和预测药物靶点-疾病关联随时间的变化。该图谱包含110,396个实体和1,110万条边（19种关系类型），每条边记录其证据发生变化的年份，从而可以恢复任意靶点-疾病对在历史决策时间点的证据画像。作者定义了一个决策对齐的基准任务，预测进入II期临床试验的靶点-疾病对能否推进到III期。基于图谱的传播方法在top-10表现上相对成功率可达4.3-4.5，显著优于直接证据参考模型，特别是在72.8%缺乏直接靶点-疾病证据的案例中，图编码器仍能以5-6倍于随机的概率恢复信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 靶点-疾病关联不充分是导致约40-50%的II期临床试验失败的主要原因。如果在进入临床时就能预判哪些项目最有可能成功，赞助方就能优先支持最有前景的假设。然而，现有的生物医学知识图谱无法按过去时间点重建支持关联的证据画像，这使得回顾性验证和前瞻性决策都缺乏基于时间的数据基础。

Method: 作者构建了THBKG时间异构图谱，每条边携带证据变更年份信息，使得可以按任意历史时间点恢复靶点-疾病对的证据子图。在此基础上定义了决策对齐的基准：仅使用决策日期前可获得的证据来预测II期到III期的推进。采用图传播（graph propagation）算法在时间限定子图上进行预测，并引入路径可解释方法将预测分解为预测背后的证据景观。

Result: 在图谱上的图传播方法在所有决策对齐协议下的直接证据参考模型中表现最优，top-10对每治疗领域的相对成功率达4.3-4.5。增益集中在72.8%完全没有直接靶点-疾病证据的案例中，这些案例中直接边模型无数据可读，而图编码器仍能达到5-6倍于随机的预测水平，通过传播跨越中间生物学路径来恢复信号。

Conclusion: THBKG为治疗靶点假设提供了可追溯时间证据的持续更新基底，支持回顾性验证，并为可解释的靶点-疾病预测提供了新的时间感知框架。该方法在无直接证据场景下仍能通过间接生物学路径传播提取信号，对未来药物研发决策具有实际参考价值。

Abstract: Inadequate target--disease linkage accounts for 40--50\% of Phase~II efficacy failures, so anticipating which programmes will advance would let sponsors back the hypotheses most likely to reach patients. What a programme can be judged on is the evidence that supported its linkage \emph{when it entered the clinic}. No existing biomedical knowledge graph allows that evidence profile to be assembled as of a past date. We present the Temporal Heterogeneous Biomedical Knowledge Graph (THBKG), which describes and predicts therapeutic target--disease links through time: 110,396 entities and 11.1M edges across nineteen relation types, each edge carrying the year its evidence changed, so a pair's profile can be recovered as it stood when its own decision fell due. On this graph we define a decision-aligned benchmark that predicts, for a target--disease pair entering Phase~II, whether it advances to Phase~III on evidence datable before that decision. Graph propagation over the THBKG outranks every direct-evidence reference scored under the same decision-aligned protocol, reaching a relative success of 4.3--4.5 at the top ten pairs per therapeutic area. The gain concentrates on the 72.8\% of pairs with no direct target--disease evidence at their decision point, where a direct-edge model has nothing to read: the encoders still rank five- to sixfold above chance, recovering the signal by propagating over the intervening biology. Adapting a path-based explainer to the decision-time subgraph decomposes each prediction into the evidence landscape behind the hypothesis for explainable prediction. We release the THBKG as a continually updated substrate for studying therapeutic target hypotheses by retrospective validation.

</details>


### [262] [Observation-Grounded Self-Predictive Reinforcement Learning for Visual Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05989)
*Xinwei Liu,Junyuan Liang,Jianting Zhang,Wuhui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OG-SPR，一种基于像素的视觉强化学习算法，通过结合多步潜在空间自预测和下一观测预测两个辅助目标，并引入轻量级适配器避免共享表示被过度约束，在28个DeepMind Control任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于动力学表征学习的视觉RL方法虽然在样本效率上有所提升，但无论是潜在空间自预测还是观测空间预测，单独使用任一预测目标在数据有限的挑战性视觉控制任务上仍表现不佳，需要同时兼顾两类预测目标的优势。

Method: OG-SPR包含两个核心辅助目标：多步潜在空间自预测和下一观测预测。针对直接对共享表示施加潜在自预测会过度约束表征的问题，引入了两个轻量级适配器来执行潜在自预测，使得共享表征既能受益于时序预测信号，又不必直接满足自预测目标。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite的28个视觉控制任务上，OG-SPR在整体性能上优于现有的自预测和观测预测类RL方法，尤其在dog和humanoid等具有挑战性的领域中提升显著。

Conclusion: 同时利用潜在空间自预测和观测空间预测的互补优势，并通过适配器机制缓解共享表征的过度约束问题，可以更有效地提升样本效率较低的视觉RL任务的学习性能。

Abstract: Sample-efficient policy learning from pixels is a long-standing challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Recent dynamics-based representation learning methods have significantly improved the sample efficiency of model-free visual RL by learning dynamics-aware representations through auxiliary prediction performed either in latent space (self-prediction) or observation space (observation prediction). However, state-of-the-art methods from both categories still struggle on challenging visual control tasks when training data is limited. We posit that relying on either predictive objective alone may be insufficient. In contrast, observation prediction grounds learned representations in observation-level dynamics, but does not directly regularize the temporal predictability of latent representations over extended horizons. In this paper, we propose Observation-Grounded Self-Predictive Representations (OG-SPR), a model-free visual RL algorithm for continuous control that learns representations that are both temporally predictive in latent space and grounded in observation-level dynamics. OG-SPR incorporates two core auxiliary objectives: multi-step latent self-prediction and next-observation prediction. We empirically show that directly imposing latent self-prediction on the shared representation may over-constrain it and does not necessarily improve performance. To address this issue, OG-SPR introduces two lightweight adapters for latent self-prediction, allowing the shared representation to benefit from temporally predictive signals without being forced to directly satisfy the self-prediction objective. Experiments on 28 visual control tasks from the DeepMind Control Suite show that OG-SPR improves aggregate performance over state-of-the-art self-predictive and observation-predictive RL methods, with particularly pronounced gains in challenging domains such as dog and humanoid.

</details>


### [263] [A Unified Risk View of Uncertainty: Posterior Risk for Disentanglement and Evaluation Beyond Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05995)
*Frieder Wizgall,Georg Tirpitz,Moritz Seiler,Kerstin Ritter,Bálint Mucsányi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将不确定性统一定义为逐点后验风险（点态预测器在合理真实函数分布下的期望损失），并以此为基础构建了一个基于半合成数据集的、可直接计算神谕（oracle）认知不确定性与偶然不确定性的基准测试框架，从而避免了传统代理任务（如OOD检测）无法提供完整真实不确定性目标的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全性敏感应用中需要可靠的不确定性估计，但文献中对认知不确定性与偶然不确定性的定义不一致，且缺乏可用的真实认知不确定性基准，现有评测多依赖代理任务（如OOD检测），无法提供完整的不确定性真实目标，也难以评估不确定性估计的结构和质量。

Method: 提出将不确定性统一定义为逐点后验风险（pointwise posterior risk），即预测器在给定数据条件下合理真实函数分布下的期望损失。该定义结合了函数上的贝叶斯不确定性以及依赖于估计器的与后验均值的偏差，能够捕捉模型设定错误和优化误差等影响。基于此定义构建理论支持的基准，使用具有真实协变量和已知生成过程的半合成数据集，直接计算神谕认知不确定性和偶然不确定性，避免代理评测。

Result: 实验发现，准确的预测并不能保证可靠的不确定性解耦。基准测试揭示了方法之间实际有用的差异，识别出与神谕不确定性目标有有意义对齐的方法，同时暴露了对数据集和建模选择的敏感性。

Conclusion: 该基准框架为不确定性估计提供了更严谨的评估方式，表明需要将预测精度与不确定性解耦质量分开评估，并为未来不确定性估计方法的设计与选择提供了理论依据和实用指导。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimates are critical in safety-sensitive applications, where understanding the sources of predictive uncertainty is essential. This often requires disentangling epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric uncertainty, yet these uncertainty types are not defined consistently across the literature, making it difficult to assess whether a method produces accurate uncertainty estimates. Evaluation is further complicated by the fact that ground-truth epistemic uncertainty is typically unavailable. Existing benchmarks therefore mostly rely on proxy tasks such as out-of-distribution detection, which do not provide complete ground-truth uncertainty targets and offer limited insight into the structure and quality of uncertainty estimates. We propose a unified definition of uncertainty as pointwise posterior risk, the expected loss of a predictor under the distribution of plausible ground-truth functions given the data. This view combines Bayesian uncertainty over functions with estimator-dependent deviations from the posterior mean, capturing effects such as misspecification and optimization error. This formulation constitutes the foundation of a theory-backed benchmark that enables direct computation of oracle epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty using semi-synthetic datasets with real covariates and known generative processes. By avoiding proxy evaluations, the benchmark enables fine-grained analysis of uncertainty estimates. Empirically, we find that accurate prediction does not guarantee reliable uncertainty disentanglement. The benchmark reveals practically useful differences between methods, identifying approaches with meaningful alignment to oracle uncertainty targets while exposing sensitivity to datasets and modeling choices.

</details>


### [264] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Agree with Themselves?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06004)
*Christian Klötergens,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Tom Hanika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出表格式基础模型（TFMs）虽然是目前表格预测问题的最佳方法，但其通过自回归方式构建的多元联合分布并不忠实于真实的后验分布。作者提出了两个一致性要求（边缘化一致性和分解一致性）来检验模型预测是否可能来自任何联合分布，并发现所有被评估的TFM都违反了这两个要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFMs是目前表格预测任务的最佳方法，但它们通过自回归方式（逐一采样目标变量并加入特征）将单变量预测器转换为多变量的做法，其联合分布的忠实性从未被研究过。由于真实数据集的真实后验分布未知，无法直接评估TFM的预测是否准确，因此需要提出另一种可验证的评估方式。

Method: 作者提出了两个任何模型都必须满足的一致性要求：（1）边缘化一致性——将条件分布进行边缘化得到的结果应与直接预测的边缘分布相等；（2）分解一致性——不同分解顺序得到的联合分布应彼此相等。这些要求不依赖于真实后验分布，仅基于概率论的数学性质，因此可以在任何数据集上验证。

Result: 在所有被评估的数据集上，无论分类还是回归任务，所有被测试的TFM都同时违反了边缘化一致性和分解一致性这两个要求。

Conclusion: 当前TFMs构建的多元联合分布存在系统性缺陷，无法忠实反映任何一致的联合分布。这提示研究者需要重新审视TFM的自回归生成机制，并可能需要在训练或推理过程中引入一致性约束来改善模型的联合分布质量。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) are currently the best approach to tabular prediction problems. They are constructed as transformers that approximate the Bayesian posterior predictive distribution based on a pre-training prior. These univariate predictors can be converted into multivariate ones autoregressively by sampling one target and adding it to the features.
  However, the faithfulness of the resulting joint has not been investigated. Furthermore, TFMs cannot be evaluated against the posterior itself, at least not on real-world datasets, because the ground-truth distribution is unknown. We therefore propose asking a different question: could a model's predictions result from any joint distribution? To answer this question, we pose two requirements that any such model must satisfy. The first is marginalization consistency, which demands that marginalized conditionals are equal to directly predicted marginals. The second is factorization consistency, which demands that different factorization orders result in equal joint distributions. Every TFM that we evaluate violates both of these requirements for both classification and regression across all datasets.

</details>


### [265] [ProDVI: Programmatic Dynamics Priors for Value Network Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06015)
*Xinwei Liu,Junyuan Liang,Jianting Zhang,Wuhui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ProDVI框架，利用大语言模型生成Python程序编码环境动力学的粗略假设，用合成转移数据预训练价值网络的状态-动作编码器，从而为强化学习智能体提供动力学感知的初始化，无需预采集数据、模拟器或元学习，显著提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习样本效率低下，主要原因之一是智能体从零初始化，需通过在线交互获取任务相关知识。现有方法依赖预采集数据集、高保真模拟器或相关任务的元学习，但这些前提条件可能难以获取甚至不可用，亟需一种不依赖这些资源的初始化方法。

Method: ProDVI利用代码生成大语言模型生成可执行的Python函数，编码关于环境动力学的粗略假设；利用这些函数生成合成转移数据；基于这些数据构建辅助动力学预测目标，在actor-critic框架中预训练价值网络的状态-动作编码器；预训练得到的表示提供动力学感知的归纳偏置，随后通过在线学习从真实转移和奖励中进行纠正。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control Suite任务上的实验表明，ProDVI能够有效提升无模型强化学习算法的样本效率。

Conclusion: 即使生成的程序可能不准确，其诱导的初始化也可以通过在线学习从真实数据中得到纠正，证明了利用LLM常识知识进行价值网络初始化是一条可行且有效的路径。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) is notoriously sample inefficient. One contributing factor is that RL agents are typically initialized from scratch, forcing them to acquire task-relevant knowledge through online interaction. Existing approaches obtain informative initializations through pre-collected datasets, high-fidelity simulators, or meta-learning over related tasks, but these prerequisites may be difficult to access or even unavailable. In this paper, we propose Programmatic Dynamics Priors for Value Network Initialization (ProDVI), a framework that leverages the commonsense and domain knowledge encoded in large language models to initialize RL agents without relying on these resources. Specifically, ProDVI prompts a code-generating language model to produce executable Python functions that encode coarse hypotheses about environment dynamics. These functions are then used to generate synthetic transitions. Based on these transitions, we construct an auxiliary dynamics prediction objective to pretrain the state-action encoder of the value network in an actor-critic framework. The learned representation provides dynamics-aware inductive biases before online RL begins. Importantly, the generated programs are used only for representation pretraining and are not required to faithfully simulate the target environment. While the generated programs may be inaccurate, their induced initialization can be corrected through online learning from real transitions and rewards. Experiments on OpenAI Gym and DeepMind Control Suite tasks show that ProDVI can effectively improve the sample efficiency of model-free RL algorithms.

</details>


### [266] [BioKD: Selective Physiology-to-Video Knowledge Distillation via Reliability Gate for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06023)
*Bojing Hou,Ruohao Li,Yitong Zhu,Hongjun Liu,Luwen Yu,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BioKD框架，一种可靠性感知的生理信号到视频的知识蒸馏方法，利用生理信号作为特权信息训练视频学生模型，推理时仅依赖视频输入，并通过样本级可靠性门控机制和渐进蒸馏策略抑制不可靠教师监督带来的负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频情感识别在模糊或社交掩盖的行为线索下表现受限，而生理信号虽可靠但部署性差。现有方法未充分解决生理教师监督中的噪声和不稳定性问题（如受试者差异、信号伪影、时间不一致性），导致跨模态知识蒸馏中产生负迁移。

Method: BioKD框架包含两个关键组件：(1) 样本级可靠性感知门控机制，自适应调节知识迁移强度；(2) 渐进蒸馏策略，实现更稳定的跨模态蒸馏。训练阶段利用生理信号作为特权信息指导视频学生模型学习深层情感表征，推理阶段仅需非侵入式视频输入。

Result: 在DEAP和AMIGOS数据集上的实验表明，BioKD在试次划分和受试者划分两种评估协议下的效价和唤醒度识别任务中均优于代表性基线。在DEAP试次级唤醒度上达到68.01%，在更具挑战性的受试者独立设置下达65.29%。进一步分析显示BioKD能有效缓解过度自信的教师错误，且优于仅使用熵加权的策略。

Conclusion: BioKD通过显式建模监督可靠性，有效解决了生理信号教师监督中的噪声问题，抑制了负迁移，且不增加推理阶段开销，无需生理信号传感和多模态同步，具有良好部署性。

Abstract: To address the limitations of video-based emotion recognition under ambiguous or socially masked behavioral cues, as well as the poor deployability of physiological signals, this paper proposes a reliability-aware physiology-to-video knowledge distillation framework, termed BioKD. The proposed framework leverages physiological signals as privileged information during training to guide a video-based student model in learning deep affective representations, while relying solely on non-intrusive video inputs at inference time. To cope with the high noise and instability of physiological teacher supervision caused by inter-subject variability, signal artifacts, and temporal inconsistency, BioKD incorporates a sample-wise reliability-aware gating mechanism together with a progressive distillation strategy. By adaptively regulating the strength of knowledge transfer, the framework suppresses negative transfer induced by unreliable physiological supervision and enables more stable cross-modal distillation. Experiments on DEAP and AMIGOS show that BioKD consistently outperforms representative baselines under both trial-wise and subject-wise evaluation protocols for valence and arousal recognition. For example, BioKD achieves 68.01\% on DEAP (trial-wise arousal) and 65.29\% under the more challenging subject-wise setting, demonstrating improved performance under a subject-independent evaluation setting. Further analyses show that BioKD effectively mitigates overconfident teacher errors and outperforms an entropy-only weighting strategy, confirming the importance of explicitly modeling supervision reliability. In addition, BioKD introduces no additional inference-time overhead relative to the same video student architecture and removes the need for physiological sensing and multimodal synchronization.

</details>


### [267] [Hybrid-Adaptive Thread Tuning to Mitigate Simulation Execution Bottlenecks in High-Performance Reinforcement Learning Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06025)
*Jiming Su,Hantao Hua,Lujia Yin,Yiping Yao,Feng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoThread，一种混合自适应线程调优方法，用于缓解强化学习推理中的模拟器瓶颈。该方法采用物理信息神经算子（PINO）作为线程数预测器，并结合有限源M/M/1排队模型约束和引导预测，通过负载感知的在线微调来补偿预测误差。实验表明，AutoThread相比静态策略平均加速比提升18.4%，吞吐量分别达到XGBoost和Reinforcer的1.7倍和1.8倍，相比最先进方法执行时间最多减少83.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仿真-在环决策系统中，强化学习推理常受模拟器端执行开销限制，工作负载高度动态且对运行时线程配置敏感。现有多线程策略难以在执行前或执行中匹配线程资源，导致资源争用、调度开销增大和吞吐量下降，亟需一种能够快速准确自适应调整线程数的方法。

Method: AutoThread采用混合自适应线程调优策略：首先通过经验分析识别任务执行时间与调度时间之比是决定最优线程数的关键因素；然后使用物理信息神经算子（PINO）作为线程数预测器，并结合有限源M/M/1排队模型来约束和引导预测，实现动态负载下的快速准确估计；此外还执行负载感知的在线微调以补偿预测误差并细化资源分配。

Result: 实验结果表明，AutoThread相比静态策略平均加速比提升18.4%，平均吞吐量分别达到XGBoost的1.7倍和Reinforcer的1.8倍，与最先进方法相比执行时间最多减少83.8%。

Conclusion: AutoThread通过结合物理信息神经算子与排队模型的混合方法，有效解决了强化学习推理中模拟器的线程配置瓶颈问题，在动态工作负载下实现了显著的性能提升和资源优化。

Abstract: In simulation-in-the-loop decision-making systems, reinforcement learning (RL) inference is often constrained by simulator-side execution overhead, where workloads are highly dynamic and sensitive to runtime thread configurations. Existing multithreaded strategies struggle to match thread resources before or during execution, causing resource contention, scheduling overhead, and reduced throughput. Through empirical analysis, we identify the ratio of task execution time to scheduling time as the key factor determining the optimal thread count. Building on this insight, we propose AutoThread, a hybrid adaptive thread-tuning method for mitigating simulation bottlenecks in RL inference. AutoThread employs a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) as a thread-count predictor and incorporates a finite-source M/M/1 queueing model to constrain and guide prediction, enabling fast and accurate estimation under dynamic workloads. It further performs load-aware online fine-tuning to compensate for prediction errors and refine resource allocation. Experiments show that AutoThread improves average speedup by 18.4\% over static strategies, achieves average throughput of 1.7x and 1.8x that of XGBoost and Reinforcer, respectively, and reduces execution time by up to 83.8\% compared with state-of-the-art methods. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/suchenjm/AutoThread.

</details>


### [268] [Dynamic Graph Prompting via Topology-Routed Mixed-Curvature Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06031)
*Quanxin Wang,Xuanting Xie,Bingheng Li,Xingtong Yu,Shuo Wang,Ruiyi Fang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CurvPrompt框架，通过维护一组曲率多样的黎曼专家和可学习的提示，利用拓扑感知门控动态路由节点-时间实例到稀疏专家子集，构建个性化的混合曲率表示，解决动态图提示中几何欠适应问题，在少样本链路预测和节点分类任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图提示方法在单一固定嵌入空间中运行，忽略了时间推移导致的局部聚类和度异质性变化会重组边曲率谱，使得最优表示几何应随局部拓扑动态演变。作者将这一未被解决的不匹配形式化为"几何欠适应"问题。

Method: CurvPrompt包含三部分：(1) 维护一组曲率多样化的黎曼专家库，每个专家配对一个可学习提示；(2) 拓扑感知门控动态将每个节点-时间实例路由到稀疏的专家子集，构建混合曲率表示；(3) 预训练阶段使用软路由建立连续的拓扑-几何映射，下游适应阶段切换到硬Top-K路由和均匀权重，以保证参数效率和训练稳定性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CurvPrompt在少样本链路预测任务上取得显著提升，同时在节点分类任务上表现稳定且一致，验证了几何自适应提示的必要性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在动态图提示学习中，表示几何需要随局部拓扑动态演化而非固定在单一空间中，几何自适应提示范式是提升标签稀缺下游任务性能的关键。

Abstract: Dynamic graph prompting freezes a pre-trained temporal backbone and adapts it to label-scarce downstream tasks using lightweight prompts. However, existing methods operate within a single, fixed embedding space. In this work, we reveal that temporal shifts in local clustering and degree heterogeneity actively reorganize the edge curvature spectrum---indicating that the optimal representation geometry dynamically evolves with local topology over time. We formalize this unaddressed mismatch as geometry under-adaptation. To overcome this limitation, we propose CurvPrompt, a topology-routed geometry prompting framework for dynamic graphs. Instead of relying on a single space, CurvPrompt maintains a bank of curvature-diverse Riemannian experts, each paired with a learnable prompt. A topology-aware gate dynamically routes each node--time instance to a sparse subset of experts, constructing a personalized mixed-curvature representation. To ensure parameter efficiency and training stability under extreme label scarcity, CurvPrompt employs soft routing during pre-training to build a continuous topology--geometry mapping, and transitions to hard Top-K routing with uniform weights during downstream adaptation. Extensive experiments across four benchmark datasets show that CurvPrompt significantly advances few-shot link prediction while delivering strong, consistent performance on node classification tasks, validating the necessity of geometry-adaptive prompting.

</details>


### [269] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了北极航运AI辅助导航中的奖励模型可靠性问题。作者对3186个AIS轨迹数据进行了受控评估，比较了线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励和潜在上下文模型三种方法。结果发现非线性奖励比线性基线提升50.9%的似然度，但添加潜在上下文反而降低16.5%性能，表明表观上的船舶行为差异主要由可观测的路由和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助导航在北极航运中需要可解释且稳健的奖励模型，逆强化学习（IRL）可恢复船舶轨迹中的奖励函数，但现有元IRL方法引入潜在上下文变量来捕捉行为异质性，却不清楚这些潜在表征是否真正恢复隐藏偏好，还是仅重新编码了观测状态中已有的信息。

Method: 在202艘船、9个北极航运季节的3186个AIS轨迹上进行受控评估，比较三种模型：线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励和基于相同非线性架构的潜在上下文模型。采用行为分析、上下文探测和预注册的特征隐藏消融实验来验证潜在上下文是否捕获真实隐藏偏好。

Result: 非线性奖励较线性基线提升50.9%的保留似然度；加入船舶特定潜在上下文使性能降低16.5%。消融实验表明表观上的船舶间差异主要由可观测的路由和环境条件解释。此外，预测准确率、路由保真度和奖励迁移产生不同的模型排名，说明单一指标不足以评估学习到的奖励。

Conclusion: 在添加每艘船的潜在上下文之前，应先测试可观测的路由、环境和船舶特征是否已能解释行为变化。这支持在安全关键领域中更可信的AI部署。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [270] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kastor方法，将确定性物理基础模型转化为高效且准确的生成式替代模型，用于加速偏微分方程求解。通过两阶段推理（大步长自回归+非因果时间超分辨率）、均值预测正则化（MPR）和空间梯度匹配，在The Well基准的多个模拟数据集上显著提升了预报精度、频谱一致性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDE求解器计算成本高昂，而标准自回归机器学习模拟器存在误差随长时程累积的问题，且难以捕捉复杂物理系统的随机性。需要一种既能保持生成模型随机性又能减少误差累积的高效替代方案。

Method: 1) 提出两阶段推理方案：大步长因果自回归模型与无因果时间超分辨率网络相结合，减少误差累积并降低计算成本。2) 提出均值预测正则化（MPR）训练目标，约束生成模型在零噪声条件下预测确定性分布均值，提升FGN和扩散模拟器的性能与稳定性。3) 引入空间梯度匹配以提升模拟精度和物理保真度（以功率谱密度衡量）。

Result: 在The Well基准的多个仿真数据集上，Kastor在预报精度、频谱一致性和计算效率方面优于对比方法。与基于Walrus微调方法论的参考相比，平均预报误差降低42.9%；在10个数据集中的8个上，方差归一化RMSE（VRMSE）优于Walrus。

Conclusion: Kastor通过两阶段推理、MPR正则化和空间梯度匹配三个关键组件，成功将确定性物理基础模型转变为高效准确的生成式替代，在保持生成多样性的同时大幅提升了长期预报的精度和稳定性，验证了该综合方法论在物理模拟加速中的有效性。

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [271] [Is Self-Pretraining really useful to improve diagnosis in medical Time Series?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06122)
*Omar Coser,Antonio Orvieto,Paolo Soda,Loredana Zollo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自预训练（SPT）对基于Transformer的医疗时间序列模型性能的影响，涵盖多模态、多变量及单变量场景。实验在三个医疗任务（康复机器人、压力检测、帕金森病检测）上验证了SPT框架下四种掩码目标的有效性，结果表明SPT一致性地提升分类准确率0-6个百分点，且深度模型获益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期证据表明Transformer架构在长上下文基准上受益于自预训练（SPT），但这一优势是否适用于医疗时间序列（包括多模态、多变量乃至简单的单变量数据）尚未得到充分验证。尤其是在数据有限的临床场景下，作者希望评估SPT是否能提升模型性能与可扩展性。

Method: 在三个代表性医疗时间序列数据集（Camargo康复机器人、Non-EEG压力检测、Gait帕金森病检测）上，对比从零训练与SPT训练的Transformer模型。SPT采用四种基于掩码的自监督目标以促进时间维度和跨模态的表示学习，并系统变化模型深度以考察容量与预训练收益的交互效应。

Result: SPT在所有数据集和配置上一致性地提升了分类准确率0-6个百分点，收益取决于掩码策略、数据集和架构。收益不仅出现在多变量设置中，在仅含简单单变量输入的场景下同样显著。且模型越深，SPT带来的改进越大。

Conclusion: SPT是一种简单且通用的策略，无需任务特定的架构修改即可提升Transformer在医疗时间序列任务上的性能，有望增强数据受限临床环境下的模型鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Inspired by recent evidence that transformer architectures benefit from Self-PreTraining (SPT) on long-context benchmarks, we investigate whether similar gains extend to multimodal, multivariate, and even simple univariate medical time series. Our objective is to assess the impact of SPT on the performance and scalability of transformer-based models across diverse medical applications, particularly under limited data conditions. We evaluate transformer architectures on three representative medical time-series tasks: rehabilitation robotics (Camargo dataset), stress detection (Non-EEG Stress), and Parkinson's disease detection (Gait Parkinson's Disease). Models are trained either from scratch or through SPT using four masking-based objectives designed to promote temporal and cross-modal representation learning, and we systematically vary model depth to examine how capacity interacts with pre-training benefits. Across datasets and configurations, SPT consistently improves classification accuracy by 0-6 percentage points depending on masking strategy, dataset and architecture, with gains observed not only in multivariate settings but also when models are restricted to simple univariate inputs. The improvements increase for deeper models that can better exploit the enriched temporal representations learned during pre-training. These findings indicate that SPT is a simple and general strategy that enhances transformer performance on medical time-series tasks without requiring task-specific architectural changes, supporting its potential to improve robustness and accuracy in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [272] [LLM Inference Under Bursty Workload Distribution: Modifying the WAIT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06135)
*Anjali Gangadhar Katageria,Shobha Rani,Raghu Nandan Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级扩展方案，对现有WAIT调度算法进行改进，使其能够适应时变的请求到达率（无需先验流量知识），通过在线估计请求强度，在突发性和动态性更强的真实流量场景下实现比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM更高的吞吐量，同时保持相当的延迟水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
